AWS SAA-C03 — Sybex Study Guide Questions with Explanations
Source: Ben Piper and David Clinton, 4th edition; user-provided PDF.
Editorial source-based review: 14 October 2026. Targeted current AWS documentation checks are linked per item.
317 questions; 1279 option explanations.
199 retained-key reviews; 38 corrected/clarified; 80 study-only.
235 eligible for scored practice. Exact duplicates retained for reading but excluded from scored selection.
Original text and keys remain visible. For flawed items, notes explain valid uses instead of inventing a uniquely correct answer.

==============================================================================
ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 1 | Bank ID: Y00-001 | Task: 2.1
True/false: The Developer Support plan provides access to a support application program- ming interface (API).
A. True
B. False

Book answer: B
Practice answer: B
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: The Business plan offers access to a support API, but the Developer plan does not.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. The Business plan offers access to a support API, but the Developer plan does not.
B: Correct. The Business plan offers access to a support API, but the Developer plan does not.

References:
AWS reference for this review — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/awssupport/latest/user/support-plans-eos.html
Official SAA-C03 exam guide — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/aws-certification/latest/solutions-architect-associate-03/solutions-architect-associate-03.html

Question 2 | Bank ID: Y00-002 | Task: 3.2
True/false: AWS is responsible for managing the network configuration of your EC2 instances.
A. True
B. False

Book answer: B
Practice answer: B
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Customers are responsible for managing the network configuration of EC2 instances. AWS is responsible for the physical network infrastructure.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. Customers are responsible for managing the network configuration of EC2 instances. AWS is responsible for the physical network infrastructure.
B: Correct. Customers are responsible for managing the network configuration of EC2 instances. AWS is responsible for the physical network infrastructure.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 3 | Bank ID: Y00-003 | Task: 2.1
Which of the following services is most useful for decoupling the components of a monolithic application?
A. SNS
B. KMS
C. SQS
D. Glacier

Book answer: C
Practice answer: C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Simple Queue Service (SQS) allows for event- driven messaging within distributed systems that can decouple while coordinating the discrete steps of a larger process.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. SNS broadcasts notifications; SQS specifically buffers work between consumers.
B: Incorrect for this question. KMS manages encryption keys, not asynchronous work queues.
C: Correct. Simple Queue Service (SQS) allows for event- driven messaging within distributed systems that can decouple while coordinating the discrete steps of a larger process.
D: Incorrect for this question. Glacier archives objects rather than exchanging application messages.

References:
Official SAA-C03 exam guide — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/aws-certification/latest/solutions-architect-associate-03/solutions-architect-associate-03.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 4 | Bank ID: Y00-004 | Task: 3.2
An application you want to run on EC2 requires you to license it based on the number of physical CPU sockets and cores on the hardware you plan to run the application on. Which of the following tenancy models should you specify?
A. Dedicated host
B. Dedicated instance
C. Shared tenancy
D. Bring your own license

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: The dedicated host option lets you see the number of physical CPU sockets and cores on a host.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. The dedicated host option lets you see the number of physical CPU sockets and cores on a host.
B: Incorrect for this question. Dedicated Instances do not expose host sockets and cores for licensing.
C: Incorrect for this question. Shared tenancy does not provide physical host visibility.
D: Incorrect for this question. BYOL is a licensing approach, not an EC2 tenancy setting.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

Question 5 | Bank ID: Y00-005 | Task: 3.2
True/false: Changing the instance type of an EC2 instance will change its elastic IP address.
A. True
B. False

Book answer: B
Practice answer: B
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: An elastic IP address will not change. A public IP address attached to an instance will change if the instance is stopped, as would happen when changing the instance type.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. An elastic IP address will not change. A public IP address attached to an instance will change if the instance is stopped, as would happen when changing the instance type.
B: Correct. An elastic IP address will not change. A public IP address attached to an instance will change if the instance is stopped, as would happen when changing the instance type.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

Question 6 | Bank ID: Y00-006 | Task: 3.2
True/false: You can use a Quick Start Amazon Machine Image (AMI) to create any instance type.
A. True
B. False

Book answer: A
Practice answer: B
Review: Corrected | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: The AMI architecture, virtualization and other requirements must match the instance type. Quick Start selection does not bypass compatibility constraints. Practice stem clarified; original retained below. Practice answer key corrected from A to B.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. The AMI architecture, virtualization and other requirements must match the instance type. Quick Start selection does not bypass compatibility constraints.
B: Correct. The AMI architecture, virtualization and other requirements must match the instance type. Quick Start selection does not bypass compatibility constraints.

Practice wording:
True/false: A Quick Start AMI can launch any instance type regardless of CPU architecture or virtualization compatibility.
A. True
B. False

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 7 | Bank ID: Y00-007 | Task: 1.3
Which S3 encryption option does not require AWS persistently storing the encryption keys it uses to decrypt data?
A. Client- side encryption
B. SSE- KMS
C. SSE- S3
D. SSE- C

Book answer: D
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: Both client-side encryption and SSE-C avoid S3 retaining the supplied plaintext data-encryption key. The intended answer is SSE-C, but the stem does not restrict itself to server-side encryption.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. Client-side encryption also keeps plaintext keys outside S3; the original question is ambiguous.
B: Study note. KMS retains managed key material for cryptographic operations.
C: Study note. S3 manages and retains the server-side encryption keys.
D: Study note. SSE-C uses supplied keys without retaining them after requests.

References:
AWS documentation: S3 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/Welcome.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 8 | Bank ID: Y00-008 | Task: 2.1
True/false: Durability measures the percentage of likelihood that a given object will not be inadvertently lost by AWS over the course of a year.
A. True
B. False

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Durability corresponds to an average annual expected loss of objects stored on S3, not including objects you delete. Availability measures the amount of time S3 will be available to let you retrieve those objects.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. Durability corresponds to an average annual expected loss of objects stored on S3, not including objects you delete. Availability measures the amount of time S3 will be available to let you retrieve those objects.
B: Incorrect for this question. Durability corresponds to an average annual expected loss of objects stored on S3, not including objects you delete. Availability measures the amount of time S3 will be available to let you retrieve those objects.

References:
Official SAA-C03 exam guide — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/aws-certification/latest/solutions-architect-associate-03/solutions-architect-associate-03.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 9 | Bank ID: Y00-009 | Task: 3.1
True/false: After uploading a new object to S3, there will be a slight delay (one to two seconds) before the object is available.
A. True
B. False

Book answer: B
Practice answer: B
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: S3 successful object writes, overwrites and deletes have strong consistency. A successful upload does not require an eventual-consistency waiting period before a subsequent read.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. S3 successful object writes, overwrites and deletes have strong consistency. A successful upload does not require an eventual-consistency waiting period before a subsequent read.
B: Correct. S3 successful object writes, overwrites and deletes have strong consistency. A successful upload does not require an eventual-consistency waiting period before a subsequent read.

References:
AWS documentation: S3 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/Welcome.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 10 | Bank ID: Y00-010 | Task: 3.4
You created a virtual private cloud (VPC) using the Classless Inter- Domain Routing (CIDR) block 10.0.0.0/24. You need to connect to this VPC from your internal network, but the IP addresses in use on your internal network overlap with the CIDR. Which of the following is a valid way to address this problem?
A. Remove the CIDR and use IPv6 instead.
B. Change the VPC’s CIDR.
C. Create a new VPC with a different CIDR.
D. Create a secondary CIDR for the VPC.

Book answer: C
Practice answer: C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: You can’t change the primary CIDR for a VPC, so you must create a new one to connect it to your internal network.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. The primary IPv4 CIDR cannot simply be removed.
B: Incorrect for this question. An existing primary VPC CIDR cannot be resized in place.
C: Correct. You can’t change the primary CIDR for a VPC, so you must create a new one to connect it to your internal network.
D: Incorrect for this question. Adding a CIDR does not remove the overlapping primary range.

References:
AWS documentation: VPC — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/vpc/latest/userguide/what-is-amazon-vpc.html

Question 12 | Bank ID: Y00-012 | Task: 3.4
True/false: The route table for a public subnet must have a default route pointing to an Internet gateway as a target.
A. True
B. False

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Corrected | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: A route to an Internet gateway defines public-subnet Internet routing. An instance also needs suitable addressing and security rules. Private-subnet NAT routing has the NAT device as its egress target. Practice stem clarified; original retained below.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. A route to an Internet gateway defines public-subnet Internet routing. An instance also needs suitable addressing and security rules. Private-subnet NAT routing has the NAT device as its egress target.
B: Incorrect for this question. A route to an Internet gateway defines public-subnet Internet routing. An instance also needs suitable addressing and security rules. Private-subnet NAT routing has the NAT device as its egress target.

Practice wording:
True/false: For conventional public Internet access, a public subnet can have its default IPv4 route target an Internet gateway.
A. True
B. False

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 13 | Bank ID: Y00-013 | Task: 3.3
Which of the following use cases is well suited for DynamoDB?
A. Running a MongoDB database on AWS
B. Storing large binary files exceeding 1 GB in size
C. Storing JSON documents that have a consistent structure
D. Storing image assets for a website

Book answer: C
Practice answer: C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: DynamoDB is a key- value store that can be used to store items up to 400 KB in size.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. DynamoDB does not run a MongoDB server.
B: Incorrect for this question. DynamoDB items have a 400 KB size limit.
C: Correct. DynamoDB is a key- value store that can be used to store items up to 400 KB in size.
D: Incorrect for this question. Images belong in object storage; DynamoDB can store their metadata.

References:
AWS documentation: DynamoDB — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/amazondynamodb/latest/developerguide/Introduction.html

B: Incorrect for this question. A GSI can be added to an existing DynamoDB table, subject to supported settings and quotas. An LSI must be defined at table creation.

Question 15 | Bank ID: Y00-015 | Task: 3.3
True/false: Enabling point- in- time RDS snapshots is sufficient to give you a recovery point objective (RPO) of less than 10 minutes.
A. True
B. False

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Corrected | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: RDS automated backups combine snapshots with transaction logs for PITR. The latest restorable time is typically within about five minutes; monitor it rather than treating it as an unconditional guarantee. Practice stem clarified; original retained below.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. RDS automated backups combine snapshots with transaction logs for PITR. The latest restorable time is typically within about five minutes; monitor it rather than treating it as an unconditional guarantee.
B: Incorrect for this question. RDS automated backups combine snapshots with transaction logs for PITR. The latest restorable time is typically within about five minutes; monitor it rather than treating it as an unconditional guarantee.

Practice wording:
True/false: RDS automated backups with transaction-log retention normally provide a latest restorable time within a few minutes, supporting a typical RPO under ten minutes.
A. True
B. False

References:
AWS reference for this review — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonRDS/latest/UserGuide/USER_PIT.html
AWS documentation: RDS — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonRDS/latest/UserGuide/Welcome.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 16 | Bank ID: Y00-016 | Task: 2.1
Which of the following steps does the most to protect your AWS account?
A. Deleting unused Identity and Access Management (IAM) policies
B. Revoking unnecessary access for IAM users
C. Rotating root access keys
D. Restricting access to S3 buckets
E. Rotating Secure Shell (SSH) key pairs

Book answer: B
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: Least privilege is valuable, but the greatest account protection depends on existing exposure. Remove root access keys, protect root with MFA, and revoke excessive permissions; the stem provides no basis to rank all actions uniquely.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. Unused policies alone grant no permissions until attached.
B: Study note. Least privilege is valuable, but the greatest account protection depends on existing exposure. Remove root access keys, protect root with MFA, and revoke excessive permissions; the stem provides no basis to rank all actions uniquely.
C: Study note. Root access keys should be removed; rotation alone retains excessive privilege.
D: Study note. Bucket restrictions protect S3 rather than all account permissions.
E: Study note. SSH key rotation affects instance login rather than IAM authorization.

References:
Official SAA-C03 exam guide — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/aws-certification/latest/solutions-architect-associate-03/solutions-architect-associate-03.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 17 | Bank ID: Y00-017 | Task: 1.3
Which of the following can be used to encrypt the operating system of an EC2 instance?
A. AWS Secrets Manager
B. CloudHSM
C. AWS Key Management Service (KMS)
D. AWS Security Token Service (STS)

Book answer: C
Practice answer: C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: KMS can be used to encrypt Elastic Block Store (EBS) volumes that store an instance’s operating system.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. Secrets Manager stores secrets rather than encrypting EBS volumes.
B: Incorrect for this question. CloudHSM is not the direct native EBS encryption integration.
C: Correct. KMS can be used to encrypt Elastic Block Store (EBS) volumes that store an instance’s operating system.
D: Incorrect for this question. STS issues temporary credentials, not storage encryption keys.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 18 | Bank ID: Y00-018 | Task: 1.1
What is a difference between a token generated by the AWS Security Token Service (STS) and an IAM access key?
A. The token generated by STS can’t be used by an IAM principal.
B. An IAM access key is unique.
C. The token generated by STS can be used only once.
D. The token generated by STS expires.

Book answer: D
Practice answer: D
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: STS tokens expire and IAM access keys do not. An STS token can be used more than once. IAM access keys and STS tokens are both unique. An IAM principal can use an STS token.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. IAM principals can assume roles and use STS credentials.
B: Incorrect for this question. Uniqueness is not the distinction; both credential sets have identifiers.
C: Incorrect for this question. STS credentials can authorize multiple requests before expiry.
D: Correct. STS tokens expire and IAM access keys do not. An STS token can be used more than once. IAM access keys and STS tokens are both unique. An IAM principal can use an STS token.

References:
AWS documentation: IAM — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/IAM/latest/UserGuide/access_policies.html
AWS documentation: STS — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/IAM/latest/UserGuide/id_roles_common-scenarios_third-party.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 20 | Bank ID: Y00-020 | Task: 3.2
You configured a CloudWatch alarm to monitor CPU utilization for an EC2 instance. The alarm began in the INSUFFICIENT_DATA state and then entered the ALARM state. What can you conclude from this?
A. The instance recently rebooted.
B. CPU utilization is too high.
C. The CPU utilization metric crossed the alarm threshold.
D. The instance is stopped.

Book answer: C
Practice answer: C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: The transition to the ALARM state simply implies that the metric crossed a threshold but doesn’t tell you what the threshold is. Newly created alarms start out in the INSUFFICIENT_DATA state.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. A threshold breach does not establish a reboot occurred.
B: Incorrect for this question. An alarm can monitor a lower threshold; high CPU is not guaranteed.
C: Correct. The transition to the ALARM state simply implies that the metric crossed a threshold but doesn’t tell you what the threshold is. Newly created alarms start out in the INSUFFICIENT_DATA state.
D: Incorrect for this question. The state does not establish that the instance is stopped.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 21 | Bank ID: Y00-021 | Task: 2.1
Where do AWS Config and CloudTrail store their logs?
A. S3 buckets
B. CloudWatch Logs
C. CloudTrail Events
D. DynamoDB
E. Amazon Athena

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Both store their logs in S3 buckets.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. S3 holds Config delivery files and CloudTrail trail log files. Both store their logs in S3 buckets.
B: Incorrect for this question. CloudWatch Logs is optional for CloudTrail, not Config's shared delivery destination.
C: Incorrect for this question. Event history is not a common log destination for both services.
D: Incorrect for this question. DynamoDB is not their standard log delivery store.
E: Incorrect for this question. Athena queries logs but does not store their delivered log files.

References:
AWS documentation: AWS Config — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/config/latest/developerguide/WhatIsConfig.html
AWS documentation: CloudTrail — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/awscloudtrail/latest/userguide/cloudtrail-user-guide.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 23 | Bank ID: Y00-023 | Task: 3.4
You want to use Route 53 to send users to the application load balancer closest to them. Which of the following routing policies lets you do this with the least effort?
A. Latency routing
B. Geolocation routing
C. Geoproximity routing
D. Edge routing

Book answer: C
Practice answer: C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Geoproximity routing routes users to the location closest to them. Geolocation routing requires you to create records for specific locations or create a default record.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. Latency routing minimizes measured latency rather than geographic distance.
B: Incorrect for this question. Geolocation selects geographic categories rather than automatically the nearest resource.
C: Correct. Geoproximity routing routes users to the location closest to them. Geolocation routing requires you to create records for specific locations or create a default record.
D: Incorrect for this question. There is no Route 53 routing policy named Edge.

References:
AWS documentation: Route 53 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/Route53/latest/DeveloperGuide/Welcome.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 25 | Bank ID: Y00-025 | Task: 2.1
You’re designing an application that takes multiple image files and combines them into a video file that users on the Internet can download. Which of the following can help you quickly implement your application in the fastest, most highly available, and most cost- effective manner?
A. EC2 spot fleet
B. Lambda
C. Relational Database Service (RDS)
D. Auto Scaling

Book answer: B
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: Lambda can process bounded image jobs, but video size, execution duration, required dependencies and traffic are unspecified. These determine whether Lambda or an EC2/container workflow is most suitable.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. A Spot fleet requires fleet management and interruption handling.
B: Study note. Lambda suits bounded image-processing jobs; execution limits must fit the workload.
C: Study note. RDS stores relational data rather than rendering videos.
D: Study note. Auto Scaling manages EC2 capacity rather than implementing image processing.

References:
Official SAA-C03 exam guide — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/aws-certification/latest/solutions-architect-associate-03/solutions-architect-associate-03.html

ASSESSMENT TEST
Question 26 | Bank ID: Y00-026 | Task: 3.2
You’re using EC2 Auto Scaling and want to implement a scaling policy that adds one extra instance only when the average CPU utilization of each instance exceeds 90 percent. However, you don’t want it to add more than one instance every five minutes. Which of the following scaling policies should you use?
A. Simple
B. Step
C. Target tracking
D. PercentChangeInCapacity

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: A simple scaling policy changes the group size and then has a cooldown period before doing so again. Step scaling policies don’t have cooldown periods. Target tracking policies attempt to keep a metric at a set value. PercentChangeInCapacity is a simple scaling adjustment type, not a scaling policy.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. A simple scaling policy changes the group size and then has a cooldown period before doing so again. Step scaling policies don’t have cooldown periods. Target tracking policies attempt to keep a metric at a set value. PercentChangeInCapacity is a simple scaling adjustment type, not a scaling policy.
B: Incorrect for this question. Step scaling responds with configured adjustments and instance warmup rather than this simple cooldown rule.
C: Incorrect for this question. Target tracking maintains a target metric rather than a fixed one-instance increment.
D: Incorrect for this question. PercentChangeInCapacity is an adjustment type, not a scaling policy type.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 Auto Scaling — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/autoscaling/ec2/userguide/what-is-amazon-ec2-auto-scaling.html
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

Question 27 | Bank ID: Y00-027 | Task: 1.1

CHAPTER 1: Introduction to Cloud Computing and AWS
Question 1 | Bank ID: Y01-001 | Task: 3.2
Your developers want to run fully provisioned EC2 instances to support their application code deployments but prefer not to have to worry about manually configuring and launching the necessary infrastructure. Which of the following should they use?
A. AWS Lambda
B. AWS Elastic Beanstalk
C. Amazon EC2 Auto Scaling
D. Amazon Route 53

Book answer: B
Practice answer: B
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Elastic Beanstalk takes care of the ongoing underlying deployment details for you, allow- ing you to focus exclusively on your code. Lambda will respond to trigger events by running code a single time, Auto Scaling will ramp up existing infrastructure in response to demand, and Route 53 manages DNS and network routing.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. Lambda executes functions rather than provisioned EC2 application environments.
B: Correct. Elastic Beanstalk takes care of the ongoing underlying deployment details for you, allow- ing you to focus exclusively on your code. Lambda will respond to trigger events by running code a single time, Auto Scaling will ramp up existing infrastructure in response to demand, and Route 53 manages DNS and network routing.
C: Incorrect for this question. Auto Scaling adjusts instance fleets; Beanstalk also manages deployment orchestration.
D: Incorrect for this question. Route 53 manages DNS rather than installing application infrastructure.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

CHAPTER 1: Introduction to Cloud Computing and AWS
Question 2 | Bank ID: Y01-002 | Task: 2.1
Some of your application’s end users are complaining of delays when accessing your resources from remote geographic locations. Which of these services would be the most likely to help reduce the delays?
A. Amazon CloudFront
B. Amazon Route 53
C. Elastic Load Balancing
D. Amazon Glacier

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: CloudFront maintains a network of endpoints where cached versions of your application data are stored to provide quicker responses to user requests. Route 53 manages DNS and network routing, Elastic Load Balancing routes incoming user requests among a cluster of available servers, and Glacier provides high- latency, low- cost file storage.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. CloudFront maintains a network of endpoints where cached versions of your application data are stored to provide quicker responses to user requests. Route 53 manages DNS and network routing, Elastic Load Balancing routes incoming user requests among a cluster of available servers, and Glacier provides high- latency, low- cost file storage.
B: Incorrect for this question. DNS routing alone does not cache application content near users.
C: Incorrect for this question. Load balancers distribute traffic among targets rather than maintaining global content caches.
D: Incorrect for this question. Archive retrieval is inappropriate for interactive content delivery.

References:
Official SAA-C03 exam guide — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/aws-certification/latest/solutions-architect-associate-03/solutions-architect-associate-03.html

Question 4 | Bank ID: Y01-004 | Task: 2.1
You need to integrate your company’s local user access controls with some of your AWS resources. Which of the following can help you control the way your local users access your AWS services and administration console? (Choose two.)
A. AWS Identity and Access Management (IAM)
B. Key Management Service (KMS)
C. AWS Directory Service
D. Simple WorkFlow (SWF)
E. Amazon Cognito

Book answer: A, C
Practice answer: A, C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes

Directory Service allows you to integrate your resources with external users and resources through third- party authentication services. KMS is a tool for generating and managing encryption keys, and SWF is a tool for coordinating application tasks. Amazon Cognito can be used to manage authentication for your application users, but not your internal admin teams.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. AWS IAM lets you create user accounts, groups, and roles and assign them rights and permissions over specific services and resources within your AWS account. Directory Service allows you to integrate your resources with external users and resources through third- party authentication services. KMS is a tool for generating and managing encryption keys, and SWF is a tool for coordinating application tasks. Amazon Cognito can be used to manage authentication for your application users, but not your internal admin teams.
B: Incorrect for this question. KMS manages keys rather than federating workforce identities.
C: Correct. AWS IAM lets you create user accounts, groups, and roles and assign them rights and permissions over specific services and resources within your AWS account. Directory Service allows you to integrate your resources with external users and resources through third- party authentication services. KMS is a tool for generating and managing encryption keys, and SWF is a tool for coordinating application tasks. Amazon Cognito can be used to manage authentication for your application users, but not your internal admin teams.
D: Incorrect for this question. SWF coordinates tasks rather than authenticating administrators.
E: Incorrect for this question. Cognito mainly handles application users; workforce federation uses IAM and directory integrations.

Question 5 | Bank ID: Y01-005 | Task: 3.3
The data consumed by the application you’re planning will require more speed and flexibility than you can get from a closely defined relational database structure. Which AWS database service should you choose?
A. Relational Database Service (RDS)
B. Amazon Aurora
C. Amazon DynamoDB
D. Key Management Service (KMS)

Book answer: C
Practice answer: C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: DynamoDB provides a NoSQL (nonrelational) database service. Both are good for work- loads that can be more efficiently run without the relational schema of SQL database engines (like those, including Aurora, that are offered by RDS). KMS is a tool for generating and managing encryption keys.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. RDS is relational and retains SQL schema constraints.
B: Incorrect for this question. Aurora is a relational engine despite its performance advantages.
C: Correct. DynamoDB provides a NoSQL (nonrelational) database service. Both are good for work- loads that can be more efficiently run without the relational schema of SQL database engines (like those, including Aurora, that are offered by RDS). KMS is a tool for generating and managing encryption keys.
D: Incorrect for this question. KMS is a key-management service, not a database.

You’ve launched an EC2 application server instance in the AWS Ireland region and you need to access it from the web. Which of the following is the correct endpoint address that you should use?

Book answer: D

CHAPTER 1: Introduction to Cloud Computing and AWS
Question 7 | Bank ID: Y01-007 | Task: 2.1
When working to set up your first AWS deployment, you keep coming across the term avail- ability zone. What exactly is an availability zone?
A. An isolated physical datacenter within an AWS region
B. A region containing multiple isolated datacenters
C. A single network subnet used by resources within a single region
D. A single isolated server room within a datacenter

Book answer: A
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: An Availability Zone comprises one or more discrete datacenters within a Region. A is closest, but its single-datacenter wording is incomplete and should not be memorized as the definition.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. An AZ contains one or more discrete datacenters; a single-datacenter definition is incomplete.
B: Study note. A Region contains AZs, rather than an AZ containing a Region.
C: Study note. A subnet occupies one AZ but is not the AZ itself.
D: Study note. An AZ is not merely a server room.

References:
Official SAA-C03 exam guide — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/aws-certification/latest/solutions-architect-associate-03/solutions-architect-associate-03.html

Load Balancing routes incoming user requests among a cluster of available servers, CloudFront maintains a network of endpoints where cached versions of your application data are stored to provide quicker responses to user requests, and AWS endpoints are URIs that point to AWS resources within your account.
C: Incorrect for this question. CloudFront distributes cached content rather than defining instance networks.
D: Incorrect for this question. Service endpoints address services rather than organize network isolation.

CHAPTER 2: Compute Services
Question 1 | Bank ID: Y02-001 | Task: 3.2
You need to deploy multiple EC2 Linux instances that will provide your company with virtual private networks (VPNs) using software called OpenVPN. Which of the following will be the most efficient solutions? (Choose two.)
A. Select a regular Linux AMI and bootstrap it using user data that will install and con- figure the OpenVPN package on the instance and use it for your VPN instances.
B. Search the community AMIs for an official AMI provided and supported by the Open- VPN company.
C. Search the AWS Marketplace to see whether there’s an official AMI provided and sup- ported by the OpenVPN company.
D. Select a regular Linux AMI and SSH to manually install and configure the OpenVPN package.
E. Create a site- to- site VPN connection from the wizard in the AWS VPC dashboard.

Book answer: A, C
Practice answer: A, C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Many third- party companies maintain official and supported AMIs running their soft- ware on the AWS Marketplace. AMIs hosted among the community AMIs are not always official and supported versions. Since your company will need several such instances, you’ll be better off automating the process by bootstrapping rather than having to configure the software manually each time. The site- to- site VPN tool doesn’t use OpenVPN.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. Many third- party companies maintain official and supported AMIs running their soft- ware on the AWS Marketplace. AMIs hosted among the community AMIs are not always official and supported versions. Since your company will need several such instances, you’ll be better off automating the process by bootstrapping rather than having to configure the software manually each time. The site- to- site VPN tool doesn’t use OpenVPN.
B: Incorrect for this question. Community listings do not establish vendor support or authenticity.
C: Correct. Many third- party companies maintain official and supported AMIs running their soft- ware on the AWS Marketplace. AMIs hosted among the community AMIs are not always official and supported versions. Since your company will need several such instances, you’ll be better off automating the process by bootstrapping rather than having to configure the software manually each time. The site- to- site VPN tool doesn’t use OpenVPN.
D: Incorrect for this question. Manual installation repeats work and introduces configuration drift.
E: Incorrect for this question. Managed Site-to-Site VPN does not deploy the requested OpenVPN software.

Question 2 | Bank ID: Y02-002 | Task: 3.2
As part of your company’s long- term cloud migration strategy, you have a VMware virtual machine in your local infrastructure that you’d like to copy to your AWS account and run as an EC2 instance. Which of the following will be necessary steps? (Choose two.)
A. Import the virtual machine to your AWS region using a secure SSH tunnel.
B. Import the virtual machine using VM Import/Export.
C. Select the imported VM from among your private AMIs and launch an instance.
D. Select the imported VM from the AWS Marketplace AMIs and launch an instance.
E. Use the AWS CLI to securely copy your virtual machine image to an S3 bucket within the AWS region you’ll be using.

Book answer: B, C
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: VM Import/Export imports a supported VM disk image uploaded to S3; the resulting private AMI can be launched. B and C are valid, but E is also a genuine prerequisite, contrary to the supplied explanation.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. SSH is not the VM Import/Export ingestion method.
B: Study note. VM Import/Export converts a supported uploaded VM image.
C: Study note. The imported image is launched as a private AMI.
D: Study note. Marketplace does not hold your private VM import.
E: Study note. Uploading a disk image to S3 is a valid VM Import/Export prerequisite; the book wrongly dismisses it.

Question 3 | Bank ID: Y02-003 | Task: 3.2
Your AWS CLI command to launch an AMI as an EC2 instance has failed, giving you an error message that includes InvalidAMIID.NotFound. What of the following is the most likely cause?
A. You haven’t properly configured the ~/.aws/config file.
B. The AMI is being updated and is temporarily unavailable.
C. Your key pair file has been given the wrong (overly permissive) permissions.
D. The AMI you specified exists in a different region than the one you’ve currently specified.

Book answer: D
Practice answer: D
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: AMIs are specific to a single AWS region and cannot be deployed into any other region. If your AWS CLI or its key pair was not configured properly, your connection would have failed completely. A public AMI being unavailable because it’s “updating” is theoretically possible but unlikely.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. Bad CLI credentials usually cause authentication/configuration errors rather than this regional image lookup failure.
B: Incorrect for this question. Normal image updating does not explain an unavailable regional AMI identifier.
C: Incorrect for this question. Local key permissions affect SSH authentication after launch.
D: Correct. AMIs are specific to a single AWS region and cannot be deployed into any other region. If your AWS CLI or its key pair was not configured properly, your connection would have failed completely. A public AMI being unavailable because it’s “updating” is theoretically possible but unlikely.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

Question 4 | Bank ID: Y02-004 | Task: 3.2
The sensitivity of the data your company works with means that the instances you run must be secured through complete physical isolation. What should you specify as you configure a new instance?
A. Dedicated Host tenancy
B. Shared tenancy
C. Dedicated Instance tenancy
D. Isolated tenancy

Book answer: A
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. Dedicated Hosts provide host allocation and placement visibility.
B: Study note. Shared tenancy can colocate unrelated customers.
C: Study note. Dedicated Instances also provide hardware isolation from other customers; the stem does not distinguish them.
D: Study note. Isolated tenancy is not an EC2 tenancy value.

Question 5 | Bank ID: Y02-005 | Task: 3.2
Normally, two instances running m5.large instance types can handle the traffic accessing your online e- commerce site, but you know that you will face short, unpredictable periods of high demand. Which of the following choices should you implement? (Choose two.)
A. Configure autoscaling.
B. Configure load balancing.
C. Purchase two m5.large instances on the spot market and as many on- demand instances as necessary.
D. Shut down your m5.large instances and purchase instances using a more robust instance type to replace them.
E. Purchase two m5.large reserve instances and as many on- demand instances as necessary.

Book answer: A, E
Practice answer: A, E
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Reserve instances will give you the best price for instances you know will be running 24/7, whereas on-d emand makes the most sense for workloads that will run at unpredictable times but can’t be shut down until they’re no longer needed. Load balancing controls traffic routing and, on its own, has no impact on your ability to meet changing demand. Since the m5.large instance type is all you need to meet normal workloads, you’ll be wasting money by running a larger type 24/7.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. Reserve instances will give you the best price for instances you know will be running 24/7, whereas on-d emand makes the most sense for workloads that will run at unpredictable times but can’t be shut down until they’re no longer needed. Load balancing controls traffic routing and, on its own, has no impact on your ability to meet changing demand. Since the m5.large instance type is all you need to meet normal workloads, you’ll be wasting money by running a larger type 24/7.
B: Incorrect for this question. Load balancing distributes traffic but does not itself add capacity.
C: Incorrect for this question. Spot interruptions are unsuitable for an uninterrupted transaction baseline.
D: Incorrect for this question. Permanent upsizing wastes capacity outside brief peaks.
E: Correct. Reserve instances will give you the best price for instances you know will be running 24/7, whereas on-d emand makes the most sense for workloads that will run at unpredictable times but can’t be shut down until they’re no longer needed. Load balancing controls traffic routing and, on its own, has no impact on your ability to meet changing demand. Since the m5.large instance type is all you need to meet normal workloads, you’ll be wasting money by running a larger type 24/7.

AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

Question 6 | Bank ID: Y02-006 | Task: 3.2
Which of the following use cases would be most cost effective if run using spot market instances?
A. Your e- commerce website is built using a publicly available AMI.
B. You provide high- end video rendering services using a fault- tolerant process that can easily manage a job that was unexpectedly interrupted.
C. You’re running a backend database that must be reliably updated to keep track of criti- cal transactions.
D. Your deployment runs as a static website on S3.

Book answer: B
Practice answer: B
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Spot market instances can be shut down with only a minimal (two- minute) warning, so they’re not recommended for workloads that require reliably predictable service. Even if your AMI can be relaunched, the interrupted workload will still be lost. Static S3 websites don’t run on EC2 infrastructure in the first place.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. A public AMI does not make an ecommerce workload interruption-tolerant.
B: Correct. Spot market instances can be shut down with only a minimal (two- minute) warning, so they’re not recommended for workloads that require reliably predictable service. Even if your AMI can be relaunched, the interrupted workload will still be lost. Static S3 websites don’t run on EC2 infrastructure in the first place.
C: Incorrect for this question. A transaction database requires continuity and durable state management.
D: Incorrect for this question. S3 static hosting does not require EC2 Spot instances.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

Question 7 | Bank ID: Y02-007 | Task: 3.2
In the course of a routine infrastructure audit, your organization discovers that some of your running EC2 instances are not configured properly and must be updated. Which of the fol- lowing configuration details cannot be changed on an existing EC2 instance?
A. AMI
B. Instance type
C. Security group
D. Public IP address

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: You can edit or even add or remove security groups from running instances and the changes will take effect instantly. Similarly, you can associate or release an elastic IP address to/from a running instance. You can change an instance type as long as you shut down the instance first. But the AMI can’t be changed; you’ll need to create an entirely new instance.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. You can edit or even add or remove security groups from running instances and the changes will take effect instantly. Similarly, you can associate or release an elastic IP address to/from a running instance. You can change an instance type as long as you shut down the instance first. But the AMI can’t be changed; you’ll need to create an entirely new instance.
B: Incorrect for this question. Supported EBS-backed instances can change type after stopping.
C: Incorrect for this question. Security group assignments and rules can be changed.
D: Incorrect for this question. An Elastic IP association can be changed.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

For an account with multiple resources running as part of multiple projects, which of the following key/value combination examples would make for the most effective identification convention for resource tags?

B. project1:server1
C. EC2:project1:server1
D. server1:project1

Book answer: B
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: AWS tags consist of a key and value, but the book convention is subjective. A stable Project key with a project-name value is also common. Multiple offered pairs are syntactically valid.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. This can be a valid tag but does not identify the project clearly.
B: Study note. This is the book's convention; AWS does not require it.
C: Study note. A tag has one key and one value, not a mandatory three-part hierarchy.
D: Study note. This can also be a valid convention; suitability is subjective.

Question 9 | Bank ID: Y02-009 | Task: 3.2
Which of the following EBS options will you need to keep your data- hungry application that requires up to 20,000 IOPS happy?
A. Cold HDD
B. General- purpose SSD
C. Throughput- optimized HDD
D. Provisioned- IOPS SSD

Book answer: D
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: Provisioned IOPS SSD and modern general-purpose gp3 volumes can both provide 20,000 IOPS on supported configurations. The source claim that only provisioned IOPS meets this target is outdated.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. Cold HDD targets throughput and cannot supply this random-I/O demand.
B: Study note. Modern gp3 also supports 20,000 IOPS, making the source key nonunique.
C: Study note. Throughput-optimized HDD is unsuitable for this random-I/O target.
D: Study note. Provisioned IOPS SSD can satisfy the target, but it is no longer the only option.

Question 10 | Bank ID: Y02-010 | Task: 3.2
Your organization needs to introduce Auto Scaling to its infrastructure and needs to gen- erate a “golden image” AMI from an existing EBS volume. This image will need to be shared among multiple AWS accounts belonging to your organization. Which of the following steps will get you there? (Choose three.)
A. Create an image from a detached EBS volume, use it to create a snapshot, select your new AMI from your private collection, and use it for your launch configuration.
B. Create a snapshot of the EBS root volume you need, use it to create an image, select your new AMI from your private collection, and use it for your launch configuration.
C. Create an image from the EBS volume attached to the instance, select your new AMI from your private collection, and use it for your launch configuration.
D. Search the AWS Marketplace for the appropriate image and use it for your launch configuration.
E. Import the snapshot of an EBS root volume from a different AWS account, use it to create an image, select your new AMI from your private collection, and use it for your launch configuration.

Book answer: B, C, E
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: The options describe alternative image-creation workflows, not three necessary sequential steps. AMI creation requires bootable snapshots, appropriate mappings and explicit image/snapshot sharing permissions; launch templates are now preferred.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. The sequence is reversed: an EBS-backed AMI references snapshots.
B: Study note. A bootable snapshot can be registered as an AMI with correct mappings.
C: Study note. CreateImage on an instance creates an AMI and backing snapshots.
D: Study note. Marketplace does not contain the organization's private golden image.
E: Study note. A shared/copied snapshot can help create an AMI; this is an alternative workflow, not a required third step.

References:
AWS documentation: EBS — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/ebs/latest/userguide/what-is-ebs.html

Question 11 | Bank ID: Y02-011 | Task: 3.2
Which of the following are benefits of instance store volumes? (Choose two.)
A. Instance volumes are physically attached to the server that’s hosting your instance, allowing faster data access.
B. Instance volumes can be used to store data even after the instance is shut down.
C. The use of instance volumes does not incur costs (beyond those for the instance itself).
D. You can set termination protection so that an instance volume can’t be accidentally shut down.
E. Instance volumes are commonly used as a base for the creation of AMIs.

Book answer: A, C
Practice answer: A, C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Instance-store storage is physically local and included with supported instance types. It is ephemeral across stop/termination or host loss, although a normal reboot retains its data.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. Instance-store storage is physically local and included with supported instance types. It is ephemeral across stop/termination or host loss, although a normal reboot retains its data.
B: Incorrect for this question. Stopping or terminating the instance loses instance-store data; reboot differs.
C: Correct. Instance-store storage is physically local and included with supported instance types. It is ephemeral across stop/termination or host loss, although a normal reboot retains its data.
D: Incorrect for this question. Termination protection applies to instances, not independent persistent instance-store volumes.
E: Incorrect for this question. Instance store is ephemeral rather than the normal durable AMI snapshot source.

Question 13 | Bank ID: Y02-013 | Task: 1.2
You need to restrict access to your EC2 instance- based application to only certain clients and only certain targets. Which three attributes of an incoming data packet are used by a security group to determine whether it should be allowed through? (Choose three.)
A. Network port
B. Source address
C. Datagram header size
D. Network protocol
E. Destination address

Book answer: A, B, D
Practice answer: A, B, D
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Ports and source and destinations addresses are considered by security group rules. Security group rules do not take packet size into consideration. Since a security group is directly associated with specific objects, there’s no need to reference the target address.

C: Incorrect for this question. Security group rules do not match datagram header size.
D: Correct. Ports and source and destinations addresses are considered by security group rules. Security group rules do not take packet size into consideration. Since a security group is directly associated with specific objects, there’s no need to reference the target address.
E: Incorrect for this question. For inbound rules the destination is the attached resource; it is not a separate arbitrary address filter.

Question 14 | Bank ID: Y02-014 | Task: 1.1
How are IAM roles commonly used to ensure secure resource access in relation to EC2 instances?
A. A role can assign processes running on the EC2 instance itself permission to access other AWS resources.
B. A user can be given permission to authenticate as a role and access all associated resources.

D. A role can give users and resources permission to access the EC2 instance.

Book answer: D
Practice answer: A
Review: Corrected | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Attach a role through an instance profile so the application obtains rotating temporary credentials. This authorizes service API requests rather than opens inbound network login. Practice stem clarified; original retained below. Practice answer key corrected from D to A.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. An EC2 instance profile supplies temporary role credentials to instance applications. Attach a role through an instance profile so the application obtains rotating temporary credentials. This authorizes service API requests rather than opens inbound network login.
B: Incorrect for this question. Role assumption is possible, but this does not specifically describe the instance profile use case.
C: Incorrect for this question. A normal instance role attaches through an instance profile, not individual Linux processes.
D: Incorrect for this question. Roles do not by themselves open SSH or RDP network access to an instance.

How is an IAM role normally used by an application running on an EC2 instance to access AWS services securely?

Question 15 | Bank ID: Y02-015 | Task: 3.2
You have an instance running within a private subnet that needs external network access to receive software updates and patches. Which of the following can securely provide that access from a public subnet within the same VPC? (Choose two.)
A. Internet gateway
B. NAT instance
C. Virtual private gateway
D. NAT gateway
E. VPN

Book answer: B, D
Practice answer: B, D
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: NAT instances and NAT gateways are AWS tools for safely routing traffic between private and public subnets and from there, out to the Internet. An Internet gateway connects a VPC with the Internet, and a virtual private gateway connects a VPC with a remote site over a secure VPN. A stand- alone VPN wouldn’t normally be helpful for this purpose.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. An Internet gateway alone does not translate a private-only instance's source address.
B: Correct. NAT instances and NAT gateways are AWS tools for safely routing traffic between private and public subnets and from there, out to the Internet. An Internet gateway connects a VPC with the Internet, and a virtual private gateway connects a VPC with a remote site over a secure VPN. A stand- alone VPN wouldn’t normally be helpful for this purpose.
C: Incorrect for this question. A virtual private gateway terminates VPN connections rather than public Internet NAT.
D: Correct. NAT instances and NAT gateways are AWS tools for safely routing traffic between private and public subnets and from there, out to the Internet. An Internet gateway connects a VPC with the Internet, and a virtual private gateway connects a VPC with a remote site over a secure VPN. A stand- alone VPN wouldn’t normally be helpful for this purpose.
E: Incorrect for this question. A VPN does not supply the requested public-subnet NAT component.

AWS documentation: VPC — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/vpc/latest/userguide/what-is-amazon-vpc.html

Question 16 | Bank ID: Y02-016 | Task: 1.1
What do you have to do to securely authenticate to the GUI console of a Windows EC2 session?
A. Use the private key of your key pair to initiate an SSH tunnel session.
B. Use the public key of your key pair to initiate an SSH tunnel session.
C. Use the public key of your key pair to retrieve the password you’ll use to log in.
D. Use the private key of your key pair to retrieve the password you’ll use to log in.

Book answer: D
Practice answer: D
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Use the EC2 key pair private key to decrypt the generated Windows Administrator password, then authenticate through RDP. This is different from Linux SSH public-key authentication.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. Windows GUI access uses RDP rather than an SSH tunnel.
B: Incorrect for this question. The public key neither starts the required RDP session nor decrypts the password.
C: Incorrect for this question. The public key cannot decrypt the generated Administrator password.
D: Correct. Use the EC2 key pair private key to decrypt the generated Windows Administrator password, then authenticate through RDP. This is different from Linux SSH public-key authentication.

Question 18 | Bank ID: Y02-018 | Task: 3.2
To save configuration time and money, you want your application to run only when net- work events trigger it but shut down immediately after. Which of the following will do that for you?
A. AWS Lambda
B. AWS Elastic Beanstalk
C. Amazon Elastic Container Service (ECS)
D. Auto Scaling

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Lambda can be used as such a trigger. Beanstalk launches and manages infrastructure for your application that will remain running until you manually stop it, ECS manages Docker containers but doesn’t necessarily stop them when a task is done, and Auto Scaling can add instances to an already running deployment to meet demand.

Option-by-option reasons:

Beanstalk launches and manages infrastructure for your application that will remain running until you manually stop it, ECS manages Docker containers but doesn’t necessarily stop them when a task is done, and Auto Scaling can add instances to an already running deployment to meet demand.
B: Incorrect for this question. Beanstalk normally maintains a provisioned environment.
C: Incorrect for this question. ECS tasks can scale, but the stem describes event-triggered functions.
D: Incorrect for this question. Auto Scaling maintains a fleet rather than per-event function execution.

Question 20 | Bank ID: Y02-020 | Task: 3.2
You’ve configured an EC2 Auto Scaling group to use a launch configuration to provision and install an application on several instances. You now need to reconfigure Auto Scaling to install an additional application on new instances. Which of the following should you do?
A. Modify the launch configuration.
B. Create a launch template and configure the Auto Scaling group to use it.
C. Modify the launch template.
D. Modify the CloudFormation template.

Book answer: B
Practice answer: B
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Launch configurations are immutable. Create a launch template specifying the new application bootstrap/image and configure the group to use it. CloudFormation can manage these resources, but none is specified in this question.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. Launch configurations are immutable.
B: Correct. Launch configurations are immutable. Create a launch template specifying the new application bootstrap/image and configure the group to use it. CloudFormation can manage these resources, but none is specified in this question.
C: Incorrect for this question. The group currently has no launch template to modify.
D: Incorrect for this question. No CloudFormation-managed deployment was specified.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 Auto Scaling — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/autoscaling/ec2/userguide/what-is-amazon-ec2-auto-scaling.html
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

CHAPTER 2: Compute Services
Question 21 | Bank ID: Y02-021 | Task: 3.2
You create an Auto Scaling group with a minimum group size of 3, a maximum group size of 10, and a desired capacity of 5. You then manually terminate two instances in the group. Which of the following will Auto Scaling do?
A. Create two new instances.
B. Reduce the desired capacity to 3.
C. Nothing.
D. Increment the minimum group size to 5.

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Auto Scaling strives to maintain the number of instances specified in the desired capacity setting. If the desired capacity setting isn’t set, Auto Scaling will attempt to maintain the number of instances specified by the minimum group size. Given a desired capacity value of 5, there should be five healthy instances. If you manually terminate two of them, Auto Scaling will create two new ones to replace them. Auto Scaling will not adjust the desired capacity or minimum group size.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. Auto Scaling strives to maintain the number of instances specified in the desired capacity setting. If the desired capacity setting isn’t set, Auto Scaling will attempt to maintain the number of instances specified by the minimum group size. Given a desired capacity value of 5, there should be five healthy instances. If you manually terminate two of them, Auto Scaling will create two new ones to replace them. Auto Scaling will not adjust the desired capacity or minimum group size.
B: Incorrect for this question. Direct termination does not automatically reduce the desired count.
C: Incorrect for this question. Auto Scaling restores the desired capacity of five.
D: Incorrect for this question. The minimum remains three; replacement satisfies desired capacity.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

Question 22 | Bank ID: Y02-022 | Task: 3.2
You’re running an application that receives a spike in traffic on the first day of every month. You want to configure Auto Scaling to add more instances before the spike begins and then add additional instances in proportion to the CPU utilization of each instance. Which of the following should you implement? (Choose all that apply.)
A. Target tracking policies
B. Scheduled actions
C. Step scaling policies
D. Simple scaling policies
E. Load balancing

Book answer: B, C
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: Scheduled actions pre-scale for the calendar spike. Target tracking and step scaling can both respond to CPU demand. The select-all source key omits a valid target-tracking policy and does not define a specific proportional adjustment requirement.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. Target tracking is also a valid demand-responsive policy, making the original key incomplete.
B: Study note. Scheduled actions add capacity ahead of a predictable spike.
C: Study note. Step scaling can add capacity in response to metric thresholds.
D: Study note. Simple scaling uses cooldowns rather than continuously matching a target.
E: Study note. Load balancing distributes requests rather than scheduling capacity.

References:
AWS documentation: EC2 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

Question 23 | Bank ID: Y02-023 | Task: 3.2
As part of your new data backup protocols, you need to manually take EBS snapshots of sev- eral hundred volumes. Which type of Systems Manager document enables you to do this?
A. Command
B. Automation
C. Policy
D. Manual

Book answer: B
Practice answer: B
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Automation documents let you perform actions against your AWS resources, including taking EBS snapshots. Although called automation documents, you can still manually exe- cute them. A command document performs actions within a Linux or a Windows instance. A policy document works only with State Manager and can’t take an EBS snapshot. There’s no manual document type.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. Command documents run commands on managed nodes rather than this resource workflow.
B: Correct. Automation documents let you perform actions against your AWS resources, including taking EBS snapshots. Although called automation documents, you can still manually exe- cute them. A command document performs actions within a Linux or a Windows instance. A policy document works only with State Manager and can’t take an EBS snapshot. There’s no manual document type.
C: Incorrect for this question. Policy documents govern managed-node policies rather than orchestrating snapshots.
D: Incorrect for this question. Manual is not this Systems Manager document type.

References:
AWS documentation: Systems Manager — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/systems-manager/latest/userguide/session-manager.html

Question 24 | Bank ID: Y02-024 | Task: 2.1
You want to launch and manage a complex microservices container workload in AWS but you want to avoid as many configuration headaches as possible, You figure you’ll be fine with whatever defaults you’re offered. Which of these platforms is your best choice?
A. Amazon Elastic Kubernetes Service
B. AWS Fargate
C. Amazon EKS Distro
D. Amazon Elastic Container Service

Book answer: B
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: Fargate removes server management but requires ECS or EKS as an orchestrator. ECS is also a sensible low-configuration orchestrator; the options mix compute and orchestration layers, and defaults alone do not select a unique complete platform.

A: Study note. EKS supplies Kubernetes and entails more orchestration choices.
B: Study note. Fargate removes server management but requires ECS or EKS as an orchestrator. ECS is also a sensible low-configuration orchestrator; the options mix compute and orchestration layers, and defaults alone do not select a unique complete platform.
C: Study note. EKS Distro supplies Kubernetes components to operate yourself.
D: Study note. ECS is an orchestrator; Fargate removes server management when used with it.

Question 1 | Bank ID: Y03-001 | Task: 3.1
Your organization runs Linux-b ased EC2 instances that all require low-l atency read/write access to a single set of files. Which of the following AWS services are your best choices? (Choose two.)
A. AWS Storage Gateway
B. AWS S3
C. Amazon Elastic File System
D. AWS Elastic Block Store

Book answer: A, C
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: EFS is the native managed shared Linux filesystem. Storage Gateway can supply hybrid NFS/SMB access but is not an unqualified low-latency cloud filesystem peer. The stem does not justify its required second answer.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. Storage Gateway targets hybrid access and caching; not an unqualified low-latency shared-cloud filesystem.
B: Study note. S3 object APIs are not a general POSIX filesystem.
C: Study note. EFS provides concurrent NFS access from Linux instances.
D: Study note. EBS ordinarily attaches within one AZ; Multi-Attach requires supported volumes and a cluster-aware filesystem.

Question 2 | Bank ID: Y03-002 | Task: 3.1
Your organization expects to be storing and processing large volumes of data in many small increments. When considering S3 usability, you’ll need to know whether you’ll face any prac- tical limitations in the use of AWS account resources. Which of the following will normally be available only in limited amounts?
A. PUT requests/month against an S3 bucket
B. The volume of data space available per S3 bucket
C. Account- wide S3 storage space
D. The number of S3 buckets within a single account

Book answer: D
Practice answer: D
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: S3 bucket counts have account/service quotas, while aggregate storage bytes and monthly PUT operations have no fixed bucket capacity allocation. Check current quotas rather than memorize the historical 100-bucket default.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. PUT requests are metered, not a fixed monthly allowance.
B: Incorrect for this question. Buckets do not have a fixed aggregate byte capacity.
C: Incorrect for this question. An account has no fixed total S3 byte capacity.
D: Correct. S3 bucket counts have account/service quotas, while aggregate storage bytes and monthly PUT operations have no fixed bucket capacity allocation. Check current quotas rather than memorize the historical 100-bucket default.

Question 3 | Bank ID: Y03-003 | Task: 3.1
You have a publicly available file called filename stored in an S3 bucket named bucketname. Which of the following addresses will successfully retrieve the file using a web browser?
A. s3.amazonaws.com/bucketname/filename
B. filename/bucketname.s3.amazonaws.com
C. s3://bucketname/filename
D. s3://filename/bucketname

Book answer: A
Practice answer: A
Review: Corrected | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: The path-style us-east-1 endpoint uses s3.amazonaws.com/bucket/key. Modern examples generally prefer regional virtual-hosted-style URLs; s3:// denotes a tool URI rather than browser HTTPS. Practice stem clarified; original retained below.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. The path-style us-east-1 endpoint uses s3.amazonaws.com/bucket/key. Modern examples generally prefer regional virtual-hosted-style URLs; s3:// denotes a tool URI rather than browser HTTPS.
B: Incorrect for this question. This is not a valid S3 HTTPS host/path arrangement.
C: Incorrect for this question. s3:// is a CLI/SDK URI scheme rather than a browser HTTPS URL.
D: Incorrect for this question. Both the URI scheme and bucket/key ordering are wrong.

Practice wording:
For a publicly readable object in a us-east-1 bucket, which listed HTTP endpoint path can retrieve the object when prefixed with https://?
A. s3.amazonaws.com/bucketname/filename
B. filename/bucketname.s3.amazonaws.com
C. s3://bucketname/filename
D. s3://filename/bucketname

References:
AWS documentation: S3 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/Welcome.html

CHAPTER 3: AWS Storage
Question 4 | Bank ID: Y03-004 | Task: 3.1
If you want the files stored in an S3 bucket to be accessible using a familiar directory hier- archy system, you’ll need to specify prefixes and delimiters. What are prefixes and delimiters?
A. A prefix is the name common to the objects you want to group, and a delimiter is the bar character (|).
B. A prefix is the DNS name that precedes the amazonaws.com domain, and a delimiter is the name you want to give your file directory.
C. A prefix is the name common to the objects you want to group, and a delimiter is a forward slash character (/).
D. A prefix is the name common to the file type you want to identify, and a delimiter is a forward slash character (/).

Book answer: C
Practice answer: C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Prefixes group keys by a common beginning, and a slash commonly represents filesystem-style hierarchy. S3 stores a flat object namespace; listing APIs can also use other delimiter characters.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Incorrect for this question. The usual filesystem-style delimiter is slash; API delimiters can be other characters.
B: Incorrect for this question. The prefix is part of the object key rather than the endpoint's DNS hostname.
C: Correct. Prefixes group keys by a common beginning, and a slash commonly represents filesystem-style hierarchy. S3 stores a flat object namespace; listing APIs can also use other delimiter characters.
D: Incorrect for this question. Prefixes group object key beginnings, not necessarily file extensions.

References:
AWS documentation: S3 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/Welcome.html

Question 5 | Bank ID: Y03-005 | Task: 1.3
Your web application relies on data objects stored in AWS S3 buckets. Compliance with industry regulations requires that those objects be encrypted and that related events be closely tracked. Which combination of tools should you use? (Choose two.)
A. Server- side encryption
B. Amazon S3- Managed Keys
C. AWS KMS- Managed Keys
D. Client- side encryption
E. AWS End- to- End managed keys

Book answer: A, C
Practice answer: A, C
Review: Reviewed | Scored practice: Yes
Rationale: Client- side encryption occurs before an object reaches the bucket (i.e., before it comes to rest in the bucket). Only AWS KMS- Managed Keys provide an audit trail. AWS End- to- End managed keys don’t exist as an AWS service.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Correct. Client- side encryption occurs before an object reaches the bucket (i.e., before it comes to rest in the bucket). Only AWS KMS- Managed Keys provide an audit trail. AWS End- to- End managed keys don’t exist as an AWS service.
B: Incorrect for this question. SSE-S3 encrypts objects, but SSE-KMS offers key-use audit events.
C: Correct. Client- side encryption occurs before an object reaches the bucket (i.e., before it comes to rest in the bucket). Only AWS KMS- Managed Keys provide an audit trail. AWS End- to- End managed keys don’t exist as an AWS service.
D: Incorrect for this question. Client encryption is possible but does not describe the listed managed server-side audit combination.
E: Incorrect for this question. End-to-End managed keys is not an S3 encryption option.

References:
AWS documentation: S3 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/Welcome.html

Question 7 | Bank ID: Y03-007 | Task: 3.1
You’re assessing the level of durability you’ll need to sufficiently ensure the long- term viability of a new web application you’re planning. Which of the following risks are covered by S3’s data durability guarantees? (Choose two.)
A. User misconfiguration
B. Account security breach
C. Infrastructure failure
D. Temporary service outages
E. Datacenter security breach

Book answer: C, E
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: S3 durability describes protection against object loss through resilient storage design. It does not guarantee recovery from every physical-security breach, account compromise or authorized deletion. C is well-supported; the claimed second guarantee is too broad.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. Durability does not undo authorized accidental deletion.
B: Study note. Durability is not an account compromise recovery guarantee.
C: Study note. Replication protects against component failure.
D: Study note. Availability concerns whether data can be accessed at a time.
E: Study note. Physical security supports protection, but durability is not an unconditional security-breach guarantee.

References:
AWS documentation: S3 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/Welcome.html

Question 8 | Bank ID: Y03-008 | Task: 3.1
Which of the following explains the difference in durability between S3’sStandard- IA and S3 Intelligent- Tiering classes?
A. Standard- IA data has only 99.9% availability, whereas Intelligent- Tiering’s availability depends on the data’s current state.
B. Standard- IA data is heavily replicated but only within a single availability zone, whereas Intelligent- Tiering data is only lightly replicated.
C. Standard- IA data is replicated across AWS regions, whereas Intelligent- Tiering data is restricted to a single region.
D. Standard- IA data is automatically backed up to Amazon Glacier, whereas Intelligent- Tiering data remains within S3.

Book answer: A
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: The stem asks about durability, but A discusses availability. Both Standard-IA and Intelligent-Tiering are designed for eleven-nines durability across multiple AZs. None of these choices states the requested durability distinction correctly.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. This describes availability rather than durability, so no option answers the original stem.
B: Study note. Both classes distribute data across multiple AZs.
C: Study note. Neither class automatically implies cross-Region replication.
D: Study note. Standard-IA does not automatically archive every object.

AWS documentation: S3 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/Welcome.html

Question 10 | Bank ID: Y03-010 | Task: 3.1
Your application regularly writes data to an S3 bucket, but you’re worried about the poten- tial for data corruption as a result of conflicting concurrent operations. Which of the follow- ing data operations would not be subject to concerns about eventual consistency?
A. Operations immediately preceding the deletion of an existing object
B. Operations subsequent to the updating of an existing object
C. Operations subsequent to the deletion of an existing object
D. Operations subsequent to the creation of a new object

Book answer: D
Practice answer: No unique defensible key assigned
Review: Review only | Scored practice: No
Rationale: S3 provides strong consistency for successful writes, overwrites and deletes. The source key relies on an obsolete eventual-consistency distinction. Concurrent updates still require application concurrency/versioning design.

Option-by-option reasons:
A: Study note. Strong consistency applies to successful S3 object operations; concurrent-write ordering is a separate issue.
B: Study note. Successful overwrites are strongly consistent.
C: Study note. Successful deletes are strongly consistent.
D: Study note. New object writes are strongly consistent too; D is not unique.

References:
AWS reference for this review — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/Welcome.html#ConsistencyModel
AWS documentation: S3 — https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/Welcome.html
